# Construction generality across the implemented UC landscape

> **Historical research snapshot.** The original text and all 89 records below retain
> the source boundary `9f9be2a89` and their original provenance. In particular, “CSP in
> progress” describes that boundary, not the current implementation. The
> [checked-CSP postscript](#postscript-checked-csp-after-the-research-snapshot) and
> [checked construction-priority postscript](#postscript-checked-construction-priorities-after-the-research-snapshot)
> record later results without rewriting or reclassifying the research records.

## Executive result

This is a **completed read-only research deliverable within the explicit corpus below**, not an implementation or a claim to exhaust all literature. The source census covers every named implemented setup/view at the supplied proof boundary, keeps proof layers separate, and explicitly includes the actual IPDL interpreter and in-progress CSP work. Six parallel semantic reports contain **89 ten-field records: 88 construction/coverage records and one canonical-source-access record**. Those records are deliberately not 88 claims of novelty: many subtract already-existing generic machinery.

The main result is that the repository's reusable baseline is substantially richer than ordinary composition: it already includes contextual kernels, setup transfer/refinement/regrading, two-sided quotients, quantitative and query-scaled composition, family/small-error collapse, rated audit/event transport, coupling/fundamental-lemma/potential rules, both game-level and machine-run deferred sampling, stochastic SSP state simulations, generic machine spans and world-indexed run-following. A new universal probability, coupling, lazy-sampling, or composition framework would duplicate real APIs.

The strongest genuinely new arbitrary-QUC candidate is small: **package security together with designated-mode availability, retaining two independent errors and compatible middle caps**. The strongest two-independent-theory work is **native typed-run/locality adapters to existing sampling APIs** (SSP–TaskPIOA, with IPDL's existing adapter preserved), followed by **test/ancilla-density interfaces for the existing ITM and SSP transfer proofs**. Larger shared work is structured: typed open-network linking, scheduler information, nominal/session allocation, and carefully separated guarded/instantaneous/stored-state feedback. None follows merely from the name UCSetup or from QUC's test-error enrichment.

### Provenance and activity

- Semantic owner/runtime: **GPT-6-Astra (`openai-codex/gpt-6-astra`)**. The six semantic slices were delegated together to pinned **m2/GPT-6-Astra** agents after scout mapping. One unavailable-provider ND attempt was retried with the same model, not silently substituted.
- Mandatory source discovery: `ImplementedSetupInventory` and `GenericConstructionCatalogue` **scouts**. Semantic interpretation, primary-literature reading, synthesis and classification were performed by Astra, not outsourced to scouts.
- Integration root: `/Users/andre/IOHK/categorical-crypto/.worktrees/uc-setup-landscape`.
- Parent-supplied source proof boundary: **`9f9be2a89`**; later docs-only revision: **`b24ae5f92`**. These identifiers and the parent's earlier check results were not independently rerun. Source-present means inspected declarations/bodies, not newly checked code.
- **No repository source/docs edits, builds, typechecks, tests, linters, formatters, Git validation, or performance measurements were performed.** Only durable local research artifacts were written.

## 1. Corpus, standardness, and completeness claim

A construction is included when an inspected primary source actually defines its operation, proof rule, theorem or counterexample, and it is relevant to a constructed carrier/source theory. A familiar name without a native contract is not enough. Protocol case studies are used only as witnesses or obstructions, not as a purported census of arbitrary encryption, MAC, commitment or MPC protocols.

Completeness has two different scopes:

1. **Repository coverage:** all declared setup/view groups in the source census have a disposition, including quantitative, negligible, observation and grade variants. Aliases and proof layers are not counted as additional native models.
2. **Literature coverage:** every inventoried foundational construction in the explicitly listed inspected sections has a classification and implementation/generalisation disposition. This is not a claim to cover every theorem in every cited book, all variants of every formalism, or all worldwide literature. The reports state their examined sections, version differences, meaningful exclusions and inaccessible sources. In particular, canonical UC full-paper access is an explicit source gap, not hidden behind an IITM or SUC substitution.

All full records use the same ten-field schema: construction/consumers/status; precise primary citation; native signature/hypotheses/laws; repository evidence/API; classification; exact instance or minimal extra data; positive/control examples; obstruction versus unproved claim; preservation/reflection/error/quantifier effects; exact proof obligations. Compact matrices below are indices into those full records, not replacements for them.

The [source census](#appendix-a) contains exact named declarations and aliases. Its closed groups are:

| Theory | Constructed setup/view coverage | Status/corpus boundary retained |
|---|---|---|
| PM | `UC.Model.Setup.StdSetup`, `QSetupᵒ`; separate `Queryᵒ` | Sealed rewrite machines; both Boolean verdict masses; query refinement is not another native theory. PR. |

| Well-bracketed | W/full, W/local, PM[wb] | Same W operational source, different test/quotient levels. No reverse full-security arrow inferred. PK. |
| Literal/relational machines | `ITM.Setup.StdSetup`; `Machine.UC.StdSetup`; `ITM.Relational.Uninitialized.UC.StdSetup` | Initialized PM interpretation, uninitialized all-state iso, and initialized helper are distinct. Finite control is not finite state or PPT. M. |
| Choice grades | NDA/NDB over PM; NDA/NDB over SSP | Four views, not four native nondeterministic operational semantics. PM lifetime request and SSP per-call FIFO picks differ. N. |
| Symbolic | fine, coarse, certified fine | Free **Mon**, not free symmetric monoidal syntax. Static/relational/run-following are proof layers. S. |

| One-shot Rel | `Examples.RelSetup.StdSetup` | Product tensor, existential relational composition, all subset tests; not probability-sensitive observation. S. |
| Auxiliary carriers | Mealy/G; SFun partial/possibility/support; free/coend constructions | **Not additional setup nodes.** PM uses Mealy/G, not SFun. Exact support abstraction is already A; setup-level security interpretation remains separate. Carrier addendum. |
| IPDL | Actual `Input/Get/Step` interpreter and SSP-distribution interpretation | **Not UCSetup/Morphism.** General typed nominal linker/source-security bridge remains open; concrete hidden-bit, identity, flattening and seeded-feedback squares already exist. IPCS. |

The actual nontrivial edges are recorded rather than reconstructed from suggestive names:

- PM→RS is present.
- W/full→PM preserves U/UC; W/full→W/local→PM[wb] are one-way observation/quotient maps.
- SSP→SSPdist preserves **and reflects** UC.
- SSP→PM[wb] and SSPdist→PM[wb] preserve UC. The existing commuting triangle is **pointwise in its specified components**, not equality of dependent morphism records and not ordinary-PM transfer.
- ITM→PM preserves **and reflects U**, preserves UC, and reflects UC only with an explicitly represented target simulator. Its forward proof uses image-dual ancillary coverage, not false PM GradeStable.
- Uninitialized ITM→machine-iso is present; initialized helper is not another setup.
- Symbolic fine→coarse, fine→machine-iso and certified fine→computational family are different arrows. The last is not automatically cryptographic soundness or full target security preservation.
- Task native readout equality and deterministic simulation→U/UC are present. Native `∀Eρ∃ρ′` contextual inclusion is not already equivalent to wiring UC.
- IPDL's finite adaptive experiment correspondence and concrete linker equalities are not setup edges.

## 3. What is already generic: the subtraction baseline

The [owner-reviewed API catalogue](#appendix-b) gives exact signatures, module/line evidence and concrete consumers. Its main families are:

| Existing API family | Actual input/contract, not an English analogy | Important boundary |
|---|---|---|
| `UCSetup`, `Abstract2` | Category C; monoidal grade category I; graded monad; test presheaf. U closes tests over ancillary grades. Dummy completeness and UC serial composition follow. | C itself need not be monoidal. No probability, state, name allocator, copying, scheduler or trace is supplied. |
| `Standard2.StdUC` | Additional monoidal carrier M and test presheaf E; grades act by tensor. | Native linking, synchronization and state laws must already make that carrier lawful. |
| Setup morphisms/transfer/refinement | Graded Kleisli morphism + natural test comparison; direct U-preservation route; epi/GradeStable and image-ancilla alternatives; source-full/faithful conditions for reflection. | Bare-kernel preservation alone fails for PM; test density is not simulator fullness. `at-image` is already public. |
| Regrading/choice/wide restriction | Monoidal grade functor; full/essentially-surjective/retract hypotheses where used; ND's partial-injective relabel realization; wide closure includes return/sub/μ/T₁, not an invented base tensor field. | Changing grade converters is not constructing powerset, demonic, convex or TaskPIOA semantics. |
| Two-sided quotient | Monoidal M, E; congruence `∀Y,Z. (idY⊗f)⊗idZ ≈ℰ (idY⊗g)⊗idZ`; quotient and descended tests. | Actual SSPdist and PM[wb] instances. Quotient equality is not UC order, and reflection is a separate theorem. |
| Quantitative witnesses/hybrids | Fixed simulator before all tests; ε⊕δ transitivity; serial error **εu⊕εf**; finite rational hybrid sum. | Error algebra need not commute. Approx-valued tests do not provide a distribution or convex structure on computations. |
| Query/family/small-error | Rated simulator and test/closure certificates; ε reindexed by `scale`; measured families; vanishing/negligible collapses and refinements. | Query cost is not time/memory/PPT. `Model.Family.Contextual.Compose` is a valid nested public module, not a missing-file defect. |
| Audit/event/potential | Rated audit transport + `Absorbs`; PM readers, same-cap protocol/PM adequacy, event lifting; per-polynomial-cap saturation; rare-event/guess/union/collision certificates. | One-sided event bounds, completed-run flag reads, prefix-hit probabilities, QUC distances and availability are different notions. |
| Game/partial coupling and defer | `StepBisim`, `JoinStep`, FLGP/hop/potential APIs; all-payoff partial bisimulation; `AvgBisim`; affine-seed `Defer.Run` at machine `Dₚ`. | Existing cores, not missing frameworks. Hop bounds are Pr1; both verdicts need the corresponding argument. |
| SSP distributional proof APIs | `Samples ρ` joint reply/next-state square, arbitrary dependent calls/external-oracle state; `sample-⟦⟧`; sampling commutations/defer; closed-game-to-U bridge. | Stochastic state map is not every possible relational coupling. Native runner/wiring/test adapters remain model-specific. |
| Machine proof carriers | Initialized pure-state simulations; coproduct-interface trace under symmetric/distributive/PureSub/Elgot inputs; relational spans; Stable/Following world APIs. | Not a product-stream trace, not raw state iso, not a UC simulator, not arbitrary simultaneous world changes. |
| Robust compilation | Outcome-only, U-hyperproperty and environment-sensitive trace characterisations; Along transfer. | A characterisation is not a compiler, native trace model, backtranslation or feasibility proof. |
| Native RS construction | Security-only `Constructs`, QRS correspondence, designated caps, security-plus-availability with two budgets. | Native availability is already implemented; only its arbitrary-QUC packaging is proposed B. |
| Free/decorated/coend/transport carriers | Actual generator interpretation, occurrence induction, coend actegory/Kleisli bridge, and transported monoidal carriers; actual Mealy/G and separate SFun effect morphisms. | Free-actegory universal adjunction is explicitly conjectured; native semantics/security does not follow from free coherence. Exact SFun support is present but lossy. |

Available dependency-level relation operators also matter: Rel dagger/converse, union and finite-path `Star/fold` already exist. Their **carrier-level** availability is A; a UC union/star/trace theorem is not thereby present. Generic free/quotient/transport, actual PM Mealy/G, and separate SFun/support details are included in G16–G19 and the [carrier addendum](#appendix-c). The existing `supp⊥` effect morphism and `SFun-supp-monoidal` must not be re-proposed as missing; their setup/test/security bridge is a separate E boundary.

## 4. Deduplicated classification matrix

**A:** already parameterized API with actual instance/available literal carrier instantiation. **B:** new consequence of arbitrary (Q)UCSetup axioms, with ordinary operation parameters. **C:** structured class with explicit additional data/laws. **D:** a specified unrestricted claim is refuted by evidence. **E:** the exact representation, converse or proof is unestablished. Categories apply to precise contracts, not whole theories: an A algebraic core, C native interpretation and D overextension may coexist in one family.

The matrix is a cross-reference index; the linked full rows supply all ten required fields and precise public primary citations.

| Deduplicated construction family | Classification and repository disposition | Minimal additional structure / exact failure | Full evidence rows |

## 5. Quantifier, equality, resource and observation audit

These are load-bearing distinctions, not terminology preferences.

### 5.1 Five independent layers

1. **Carrier equality** may be free syntax, state-isomorphism, a simulation closure, or a quotient. It is not automatically the observation relation.
2. **Bare kernel** `≈ℰ` compares the chosen tests. PM has an explicit bare/U separator and false GradeStable.
3. **U agreement** adds every ancillary grade/context. The two-sided monoidal quotient additionally pads on both sides for tensor congruence.
4. **UC order** adds a simulator converter: `∀a.∃s.∀W,test`. Dummy form fixes `s₀` before all tests. A coupling, state relation, native FSim or context-specific schedule is not that converter.
5. **Native security** also fixes interface, admissibility/runtime, scheduler, state/initialization and observation semantics. A formal setup morphism without coverage is not a completed native computational theorem.

### 5.2 Error scopes which must not be exchanged

Suppressing the common comparison predicate:

| View | Actual existential scope |
|---|---|
| Canonical local-negligible UC | `∃s. ∀W,e,m. ∃ negligible δ. ∀i` |
| Small-image negligible UC | `∃s. ∀W,e. ∃ negligible δ. ∀m,i` |
| Stronger global quantitative witness | `∃s,δ. negligible δ ∧ ∀W,e,m,i` |
| Capped event saturation | `∀ polynomial p. ∃ negligible ν. ∀n, all contexts under cap p(n)`; ν may depend on p |
| Native Task contextual implementation | `∀E,ρ. ∃ρ′. equality of the whole matched trace distribution`; ρ′ precedes all reward tests, but follows E,ρ |
| IPDL approximate observation | Negligible witness may depend on the admitted PPT context/distinguisher; realization separately fixes Sim first |
| Symbolic supplied backtranslation | One extraction/sampling/context package per feasible context **before honest f**; errors are then protocol-local; source dummy simulator fixed before contexts |
| QRS→PM closed-slack security | One s before every positive δ: `∃s.∀δ>0. At s (ε+δ)`; not `∀δ.∃s`, nor automatically exact PM error ε |

The elementary diagonal `d_m(n)=1` exactly when n=m is a concrete logical local/uniform obstruction, **not a claimed fully represented PM contextual separator**. The Rel two-branch union example, by contrast, is a concrete construction in the actual one-shot carrier showing separately selected simulators cannot generally be consolidated.

For query-aware transitivity the error is `ε₁(q)+ε₂(scale q r)`, not unscaled addition. Measured serial composition additionally certifies the actual first real process and simulated continuation. Numerical error, task-depth grade, interface/choice-site grade, query count, runtime and retained-memory bits are distinct indices.

### 5.3 Absence of output is not one universal effect

- PM/SSP observe **both false and true masses**; false and divergence/failure are distinguishable. Per-verdict ε bounds imply at most2ε failure-mass difference, not automatically ε.
- Task disabled tasks are successful **stutters**, retaining normalized probability, not aborts.
- IPDL pending `Get=None` is an ordinary successful reply, read **before** that turn's quiescence; it is not SSP's outer cemetery outcome.
- CSP distinguishes termination, deadlock, divergence and refusal according to the selected relation. Weak bisimulation can identify divergence and deadlock; FD/FSim do not have interchangeable laws.
- Prefix-hit probability can retain a witnessed bad prefix even if a later continuation dies; completed-run flag observation can be zero. Existing code contains the bad-then-dead separator.
- A future useful Task QUC cannot bound all unrestricted rational reward functions by one finite absolute ε: scaling a distinguishing reward scales its discrepancy. Bound rewards or track their norm.

## 6. Strongest reusable constructions and exact proof obligations

All new proposals below are **[INFERENCE]**, not implemented or typechecked claims. [The full candidate artifact](#appendix-e) gives R1–R7 in detail.

### Priority 1 — B: designated-cap, two-budget security

For arbitrary QUC, supplied grade maps `a:X→I`, `b:Y→I`, and protocols `f:A→T_X B`, `g:A→T_Y B`, define

`Avail_ε(a,b;f,g) := (sub a ∘ f) ≈ᵁ[ε] (sub b ∘ g)`

and pair it with `Witness εs f g`. Both capped terms have unit grade; this needs neither a tensor on C nor `T_I B≅B`, and does not assert caps exist for every object.

Same-middle-cap transitivity has errors `(εs⊕δs, εa⊕δa)` by existing `at-trans` and `ctx-trans`. Hom-equal caps add no cost; an explicit middle-mode comparison of cost κ yields availability error `εa⊕κ⊕δa`. A mode-preserving simulator satisfying `a∘s≈b` also turns its security bound into the matching availability bound.

**Consumers:** actual PM/QRS and quantitative families. **Exact remaining proof:** package the typed short derivation; prove equivalence with native RS using QRS full abstraction and two-way transport through its invertible standard unitor; preserve cap interpretation under any compiler. **Control:** existing ideal resource under false/true caps yields two perfect premise edges with incompatible middle modes but a false conclusion. Security-only counterexample is already present. No fairness or arbitrary liveness claim is implied.

### Priority 2 — C: native-run/locality adapters, not another coupling framework

Reuse `SSP.Dist.Kernel.Samples/sample-⟦⟧`, existing all-payoff partial bisimulation, `AvgBisim`, `Defer.Run` and FLGP/potential APIs. Supply a typed native oracle, finite experiment→`Calls` translation, initializer law and exact run square. `Samples` already preserves the **joint reply and final state**, including external-oracle state.

**Two independent constructed consumers:** SSP and TaskPIOA. For Task, use task as query and optional output as reply; swap pair order and embed total distributions into `Dist⊥`. Disabled tick is successful `(state,None)`, not failure. Prove fixed-word trace/payoff adequacy and receiving/input/product locality for stochastic state maps; deterministic simulation already has its source-specific security bridge.

**Third source consumer:** IPDL already has `resource/compileTree/execution/agreement`; preserve and reuse that adapter rather than re-propose it. General stochastic residual/linker closure is separate.

**Controls:** copied versus independently resampled hidden bit; wrong initialization; outer failure versus successful no-output; aliased state. **UC obligation:** native run equality upgrades only through each model's own wiring/test/ancilla closure. **Approximate obligation:** source query/potential bound and both-verdict bridge before reusing arithmetic. Existing PM event adequacy is A, not missing work.

### Priority 3 — C: test-density/ancilla-basis interfaces for transfer

Reuse `transfer⁺`, public `at-image` and existing essential-surjectivity results. Expose actual translated test/ancilla data and evaluation equations, not a new record whose only premise is the desired U-preservation conclusion.

**Independent consumers:** SSP→PM[wb] finite-test density and ITM→PM image-dual coverage; W/full inclusion is a third. Where approximation is used, construct a common source test with arbitrarily small slack and invoke the target's precise closed/all-positive rule. Keep the simulator fixed before test and slack.

**Controls:** PM's bare/U separator; SSP unsolicited-return identity separator; observation density without simulator fullness. **Remaining obligations:** factor the real basis/translation construction, retain its closure semantics, and separately supply simulator representatives for reflection. Do not claim a fixed-ε theorem from an all-positive equality-density proof.

### Larger C research packages, with genuinely different native consumers

| Package | Consumers and shared data | Exact positive obligation / falsifier |
|---|---|---|
| R4 Typed open-network linking | IPDL and CSP **source theories**; CSP not yet an integrated setup. Typed boundaries/private aliases/admissible gluing can be shared; dynamics cannot. | Prove native flattening/identity/tensor/connection-order execution squares and closing-test naturality. IPDL demand-order leak, CSP cross-sync and Matt connection-order example are controls. |
| R5 Scheduler information | Actual TaskPIOA, PM lifetime choice, SSP queued picks | Represent allowed information and prefix-consistent schedule maps; retain shuffle/order and disabled semantics; prove product/context closure. Do not infer a uniform UC simulator from `∀Eρ∃ρ′`. |
| R6 Nominal/session allocation | Symbolic roles and native IPDL; related SSP locations/ITM sessions | Distinguish arbitrary substitution from injective fresh renaming, prove binding/scope/ownership/dispatch. Dynamic replication adds actual population/runtime and single-simulator closure. Capture and shared-state correlation are controls. |
| R7 Guarded/instantaneous/stored-state feedback | Existing PM machine trace, IPDL cyclic networks, CSP guarded iteration; Rel product/sum traces are useful controls | Keep three signatures separate; instantiate existing trace when appropriate. Prove guard/initializer/context laws and finite-use or contractive error bounds. Negation, delay, blocked cycle and amplified Bernoulli error prevent a universal merger. |
| Coherent stochastic extension/conditioning/amplification | PM/RS represented fragments and finite SSP/Task kernels are prospective consumers | Construct supported-prefix common-mass/conditional data; prove native totality and joint laws; independence/neutralization and per-component usage before product bounds. Existing coupling/defer proofs remain A. |

## 7. Concrete control register and evidence strength

These controls are part of the research result, not suggestions to suppress troublesome observations:

- **Source declarations with existing proofs:** PM bare-kernel/U separation; SSP ND B→A pick-copying nonreflection; initialized/uninitialized ITM separation; SSP→SSPdist UC reflection and leaky counterexample; PM event hidden-ghost/bad-then-dead; Rel environment-blind robustness counterexample. They were inspected, **not rerun**.
- **Inspected primary native counterexamples/theorems:** Task bare trace inclusion not contextual; Task Definition-5 simulation incomplete (Early/Late)—**not** a counterexample to the unproved Contextual→wiring-UC converse; ordinary P/D Beck-law no-go; Varacca naive-bind nonassociativity; metric tensor/non-unary-logic boundaries; symbolic unindexed replication; arbitrary-equational-theory decision failures; runtime/composition and memory/reset limits; global-CRS/JUC limits.
- **Pinned CSP evidence:** cross-alphabet synchronization counterexample is source-formal; finite-visible-support `FinBr` still permits unbounded internal fan-out. The hiding FD counterexample's denotational half is a **mathematical source argument**, while its FSim obstruction is formalized in the pin. No source check was run. General fan-in DR congruence is **E**, not refuted merely by a scope note.
- **Explicit elementary deductions, marked [INFERENCE]:** two secure Rel branches with different simulators have insecure union; biased pad with full support has unavoidable≥1/4 probability error against an input-independent ideal simulator; hidden-bit peeking changes success1/2→1; repeated ε trials give `1−(1−ε)^n`; local negligible diagonal; false/true incompatible cap composition; copied coin versus independent samples; capturing substitution; shared-cell write order.
- **Universal-axiom control [INFERENCE]:** unit grade, identity monad and constant terminal tests make any category a UCSetup (and a trivial Approx enrichment a QUCSetup). A discrete monoidal category of natural numbers under addition has no1→0 or1→2, so total discard/copy cannot be derived. A free monoidal generator `A⊗U→B⊗U` need not yield `A→B`. Even total Cartesian feedback would force a Boolean-negation fixed point. These are actual countermodels to unrestricted structural claims, not merely absent fields.

## 8. Open boundaries and source-access limits

E is not used to hide a completed negative analysis; it records the following specific unresolved claims:

1. Full native random-system representation in the current PM language, and effective/coherent maximal flagged-extension realization for a chosen fragment.
2. Arbitrary target-simulator lifting for ITM or SSP compilers; ordinary-PM preservation by a **different** lawful SSP representation. The current raw identity failure does not prove every alternate compiler impossible.
3. Native finite Task contextual implementation→wiring UC, with uniform scheduler/simulator representability and ancillary test coverage. Simulation incompleteness and logical quantifier nonimplication do not settle that exact converse.
4. Multi-sorted symbolic emitter converse, full applied-pi representation, concrete cryptographic backtranslation/feasible-adversary closure, and native PPT interpretation of literal ITM. Supplied records are not implemented extractors.
5. General typed/hygienic IPDL linking/source setup/security and the in-progress CSP native network carrier/correspondence. Existing concrete IPDL squares and finite adaptive adequacy are not missing.
6. CSP fan-in DR congruence, removal of Sep in the stated stronger FD interchange question, and a compatible up-to-context instance. Positive pinned results retain their exact process and inherited classical assumptions.
7. Unrestricted two-Following composition without a common-world synchronization theorem. No counterexample was established to that exact well-typed extension.
8. Canonical UC2020 full-paper and SUC-full proof access: authoritative endpoints failed and browser support was unavailable; no uninspected theorem numbers were invented. Pous up-to positive contract is grounded in the author's versioned formal source, not an uninspected article theorem number.
9. Constructive CryptHOL CSF2019 printed Theorem4 has a visually confirmed apparent bound-index discrepancy. PK15 records the printed statement and a separately labelled safe outer-bound inference. Historical mechanized correspondence was not verified; unambiguous Theorems5–6 remain usable.
10. The universal free-actegory adjunction is explicitly a **Conjecture** in its source header. The coend category/action, displayed inverse maps and graded-Kleisli strong-equivalence bridge are actual implemented infrastructure; only the stated universal property is open.

The strongest next mathematical work is therefore not another generic UC theorem. It is the small B cap package plus narrow, constructor-faithful C adapters and representation/coverage proofs, with the listed controls retained. This report itself contains no implementation request or promise, and reports no unexercised verification.

## Embedded common research contract

## Literature construction/generalisation research — common contract

Owner runtime: openai-codex/gpt-6-astra. Semantic delegates must be explicitly pinned m2 (GPT-6-Astra); scout work is repository mapping only. Research only: no source/docs edits, no builds/typechecks/tests/linters. Integration root: /Users/andre/IOHK/categorical-crypto/.worktrees/uc-setup-landscape (reported base 9f9be2a89). Current docs/uc-models.typ and landscape are being edited; use stable code snapshots and mapping artifacts. Existing prior reports are background and may have stale status: actual IPDL interpreter, TaskPIOA setup, ITM transfer, SSPdist compiler triangle now exist. CSP is in progress in isolated worktree; no checked landing. Causal boxes and RSIM remain planned comparison theories, not constructed nodes.

### Goal
For every assigned implemented setup node/grouped source theory: identify standard operational, proof-principle and security constructions in a bounded, explicit primary-source corpus; exclude those already instances of repository generic APIs from the *new* construction list, but list them as A for coverage. Classify remaining constructions by genuine generality. Do not claim literally all worldwide literature has been exhausted. Define standardness by a named primary foundational/canonical source with an actual definition/theorem, not speculative names or arbitrary cryptographic protocol enumeration.

### Mandatory row schema
1. Stable row ID; construction name/family; consumer models and exact source/setup status.
2. Primary source precise definition/theorem/figure/section (number and inspected version); public citation URL; archival path/range when available. Never invent a theorem number.
3. Native signature, input data, hypotheses, output and laws (distinguish category, effect/grades, tests and native observation).
4. Repository implementation state: actual declaration/path/range; already present, source-specific only, absent in searched modules, or in-progress. No code check claims.
5. A/B/C/D/E classification: A already generic (exact API + actual parameter instantiation/consumer); B follows from arbitrary UCSetup/QUCSetup axioms but not already packaged; C structured class with explicit extra operations/data/laws; D a proposed unrestricted/native generalisation fails with evidence; E genuinely unresolved. C and D may be paired for a valid restricted construction and its invalid extension, but make that distinction explicit.
6. For A: exact existing API instantiation. Otherwise: precisely what UCSetup/QUCSetup lacks; minimal proposed structural assumptions, never the desired conclusion itself as the only assumption.
7. Positive examples (prefer two constructed-theory consumers), nonexamples/control witnesses.
8. Concrete obstruction/counterexample and its scope, versus merely unknown proof. Label new mathematical deductions [INFERENCE].
9. Security preservation/reflection/error effects where meaningful; exact simulator/environment/error quantifier order. Native simulation/bisimulation is not a UC simulator converter. Hom equality, test equality, bare kernel, ancilla-closed U kernel, and UC emulation must remain separate.
10. Concrete research/proof obligations to obtain a generic construction/lemma; distinguish representation theorem, closure theorem, test backtranslation, ancilla coverage, grade/simulator fullness and quantitative nonexpansiveness.

### Cross-family hypotheses to test, not assume
Traced vs guarded vs stored-state feedback; semilattice/convex choice and distributivity; scheduler information/causality/fairness; copy/discard/local/shared state; nominal freshness/alpha-equivalence; metric/convex/coupling structure; test-density/context lemmas; resource converters and wrapper/session separation. Compare signatures, not shared English names.

QUC/RS availability and error accounting, query-aware resource consumption, family vanishing vs negligible observations and local vs uniform errors are mandatory. QUC supplies error enrichment of *tests*, not necessarily probabilities/couplings/convexity on carriers. Arbitrary UCSetup does not even require a monoidal base category: only its grade category is monoidal. Standard2 is an extra monoidal-carrier specialisation.

### Corpus and output
Inspect primary texts in docs/kb/sources/papers and public primary sources as needed. Record core sources examined per model; record meaningful exclusions. Read-only repo investigation with unknown files must use scout; known mapped modules may be inspected directly. No need to repeat broad mapping.

Return a durable construction-generality-report.md report with full rows, a source coverage table, and a concise result. Prioritise actual contracts over volume. If a law is unavailable or a paper version cannot be verified, classify that exact claim E rather than invent evidence. Finish assigned synthesis; no progress-only final.

# Appendix B — Actual generic construction catalogue

# Actual generic construction catalogue

Evidence root: `.worktrees/uc-setup-landscape/src`; source proof boundary 9f9be2a89, docs-only current head b24ae5f92 (parent-confirmed). Read-only scout mapping is at `agent://ConstructionGeneralityAstra.GenericConstructionCatalogue`; its initially overbroad line endpoints/commit-discrepancy wording were corrected in a follow-up. Key contracts were directly re-read by the Astra owner. No code verification was run.

## G1 — Universal setup/UC core

`CategoricalCrypto/UCSetup.agda:21–26` requires exactly category C, monoidal grade category I, graded monad M, Setoid-valued test presheaf E. `UCSetup._∙_` (`:55–57`) gives `h:B→T_Y C`, `f:A→T_X B` ↦ `ext_X h∘f:A→T_(X⊗Y) C`. `Abstract2` supplies ancilla-closed `≈ᵁ`, `≤UC`, dummy completeness, reflexivity/transitivity and `UC-compose` (`Abstract2.agda:45–221`).

The exact security quantifiers are `∀a:X→X′. ∃s:Y→X′. sub(a)∘f ≈ᵁ sub(s)∘g`; U agreement quantifies every ancillary grade and every admitted test. `≤UC⇒dummy` extracts a single `s₀:Y→X`, and `dummy-complete` answers arbitrary a by a∘s₀. The simulator is not allowed to depend on the test. This is the exact API reused by all named setups; calling a native product/linker 'composition' does not prove its interpretation satisfies these categorical laws.

`≤UC-sub` is **not arbitrary restriction**: it needs c:X→Y, r:Y→X with r∘c≈id (`Abstract2.agda:223–252`). `GradeStable` is a derived extra property (`UCSetup.agda:144–175`), not a field. PM explicitly refutes it with `reveal-≈ℰ` and `¬gradeStableᵒ` (`UC/RandomSystems/FullAbstraction.agda:186–190`).

## G2 — Standard monoidal-carrier setup

`Standard2.StdUC`/`StdSetup` (`Standard2.agda:19–34`) takes a monoidal machine category M and a lawful test presheaf and uses tensoring as graded effects. This is an existing **structured** factory, not evidence that arbitrary UCSetup has a tensor on C. Direct named instances include PM, RS, SSP, SSPdist, W variants, ITM, symbolic, machine-iso and Rel. TaskPIOA also uses **Standard2**, imported under the local alias `Standard` (`TaskPIOA/Setup.agda:23,95`); the alias must not be mistaken for the older module. Native category/evaluator laws remain each model's obligation.

## G3 — Formal setup morphisms, image factorisation and their composition

`UCSetupMorphism` contains a `GradedKleisliMorphism` (F, grade functor Φ, κ and return/ext/sub compatibility) plus `ν:PresheafMorphism F E E′` (`UCSetup/Morphism.agda:31–39`). Protocol translation is `Gf=κ∘Ff`. `changeKernel` keeps computation/grade/effect data and accepts a natural test map; `idᵁ`, `composeᵁ` exist (`:48–68`). `Factorization.imageSetup`, `coarsen-to-image`, `image-morphism` factor tests through the image (`:101–114`). They do not prove native model correspondence by themselves.

`Standard2.Morphism.StdUCMorphism` builds the effect fields from a **strong monoidal** F and a presheaf morphism ν (`Standard2/Morphism.agda:27–58`). This is the actual API for ITM→PM, SSP compilers and fine symbolic interpretations.

## G4 — Preservation/reflection and ancillary coverage

`Abstract2.Morphism.Transfer.transfer⁺` (`:62–71`) accepts `Preserves-≈ᵁ` and derives UC preservation. Sufficient route: ν.Epi gives bare-kernel preservation, plus **target** GradeStable gives U preservation (`:51–76`). Reflection requires grade-functor fullness, κ monicity at observation, ν.Mono and **source** GradeStable (`:83–108`).

For standard setups, `surjective⇒preserves-≈ᵁ` instead takes essential surjectivity of the strong monoidal F plus bare-kernel preservation (`Standard2/Morphism.agda:96–103`), using `at-image` (`:64–75`). It avoids false target GradeStable. Current ITM transfer uses a still-more-specific image-dual/context argument (`ITM/Transfer.agda:63–105`); W/full inclusion uses essential-surjectivity coverage. SSP density supplies a concrete target-test argument. These are actual distinctions, not interchangeable hypotheses. Target-test density does not imply grade/simulator fullness.

## G5 — Same-carrier reobservation/refinement

`reobserve` changes only E. `Refine` takes the bare-kernel implication for the *same arrows and computational fields* and proves `≈ᵁ-refine`, `≤UC-refine` with the same simulator; no GradeStable needed (`Abstract2/Morphism.agda:114–143`). Direct consumers include family quantitative/negligible bridges and observation coarsening. Structural test maps and native contextual soundness are separate requirements.

## G6 — Regrading, relabeling and computational restriction

`Abstract2.Regrade.regraded` precomposes the graded monad by a supplied monoidal functor ι:J→I and supplies `inclusion` (`:45–64`). Restriction of UC needs fullness; unrestriction uses stated split/essential-surjectivity or grade-stability hypotheses (`:88–145`). `Between.between` compares grade presentations using a monoidal natural isomorphism; reflection adds fullness (`:149–220`). ND A/B instantiates these; no native nondeterministic semantics follows from the grade names.

`Abstract2.WideSubcategory.SetupSubcategory` (`:53–61`) has exact closure fields **W-id, W-∘, W-return, W-sub, W-μ, W-T₁**. It does not assume a base tensor that arbitrary C lacks. `Restriction` adds E′ and ν:PresheafMorphism ι E′ E, then builds `subSetup` and inclusion (`:106–136`); `coarsen` accepts a test map from pulled-back ambient tests to E′ (`:146–152`).

## G7 — Two-sided contextual quotient

`Standard2.UKernel._∼_` is `∀Y Z. (id_Y⊗f)⊗id_Z ≈ℰ (id_Y⊗g)⊗id_Z` (`:34–35`). Its `uKernel:MonoidalCongruence M` proves composition/tensor closure without braiding (`:61–103`), and `∼⇒≈ℰ`, `∼⇒≈ᵁ` follow (`:105–125`). Existing consumers SSPdist and PM[wb] instantiate the monoidal quotient, with descended tests. A bare U-kernel is not automatically the same two-sided monoidal congruence, and quotienting observations cannot repair a compiler whose raw identity/composition fails before lawful descent.

## G8 — Quantitative observation and fixed-simulator witnesses

`QUCSetup E` requires an ordered error algebra and an Approx-valued presheaf, preserving C/I/M (`UC/Quantitative.agda:40–70`). `underlying₀`, `underlying₊` (requires Refinement E) and `underlyingSmall` (requires SmallClass) are distinct qualitative forgetful views. Approx structure lives on **tests/observations**, not necessarily stochastic carrier arrows.

`≈ᵁ[ε]` quantifies W and tests at one fixed ε; `ctx-trans` costs ε⊕δ; pullback and simulator absorption are nonexpansive (`UC/Quantitative.agda:112–186`). `At s ε f g=f≈ᵁ[ε]sub(s)g`; `Witness ε f g=∃s.At`; `Witness⁺` is attack-universal and dummy-equivalent at fixed error (`UC/Quantitative/Witness.agda:41–61`). `at-trans` composes s∘t and ε⊕δ; `at-compose` uses `(id⊗t)∘(s⊗id)` and **ε_u⊕ε_f in that order** (`:66–80`). Do not silently commute a generic error algebra. Concrete consumers PM/QRS and family QUC views.

## G9 — Hybrid, rate/query and family machinery

`Approx.Rational.hybrid` (`:95–99`) already sums a finite chain's per-step errors for a supplied approximation. Repeated calls inside an adaptive context need a separate usage/query argument before this arithmetic applies.

`UC.QueryBound.Certificate.QBᵢ` supplies potential, initialization/step coherence and per-query bounds; compose/tensor laws feed `UC.Machine.Grading` (`UC/QueryBound.agda:38–158`). This is concrete Proc/machine resource structure, not PPT semantics or a generic consequence of a numerical error grade.

Above that concrete certificate layer, **generic query-aware** `UC.Quantitative.Contextual` takes a monoidal M, a Rates-graded subcategory Rg, a rational Approximation and lawful readout (`:48–55`). Its `ctx-sub` requires a simulator certificate `Image forget r s` and transforms the error schedule to `q↦ε(scale q r)` (`:192–200`); `at-trans` produces `q↦ε₁(q)+ε₂(scale q r)`, not pointwise ε₁+ε₂ (`:202–216`). `absorb-testᵠ` and `absorb-closureᵠ` keep test and closure resource use separate (`:145–172`). This is an existing structured generic API, distinct from fixed-error QUC nonexpansiveness. The concrete `UC.Model.Family.Contextual.agda:21–23` publicly instantiates `UC.Quantitative.Family` at `𝔾ᵒ`, `qevaluationᵒ`, `gradingᵒ` and the PM observation laws; its imported `Compose` is a nested module, not a missing `Compose.agda` file. Cite actual declarations rather than infer staleness from dotted module names.

`UC.Family` requires a monoidal/readout basis, quantitative evaluation, rate-graded subcategory, index and cofinal security projection κ. It constructs Famᴹ and the eventual/vanishing setup (`:69–149`). Measured family agreement retains closure/query certificates and an error function; absorption needs `VanishingBound` (`:144–273`). `Approx.Small` collapses an additively closed small error class. `UC.Approximate.Local` is explicitly an **instance** of that collapse, not a new generic construction (`:3–19,52–74`). At the **test-comparison** level, canonical negligible equality has `∀closure m.∃negligible δ.∀i`; the small quantitative image has `∃negligible δ.∀m,i`. At the **UC** level both retain one simulator first, but `setupᴺ⁺` only gives `∃s.∀W,e.∃δ.∀m,i`, whereas canonical local gives `∃s.∀W,e,m.∃δ.∀i`. A genuinely global quantitative witness is stronger: `∃s,δ.∀W,e,m,i`. `Bridge.Small.small-emulation⇐` and `uniform-witness⇒≤UCᴺ` go from that stronger input to UC; they do not uniformly extract δ back out (`UC/Quantitative/Bridge.agda:118–145`; `UC/Family/Negligible/Quantitative.agda:81–113`). No uniformization or PPT characterisation follows.

## G10 — Actual machine-carrier feedback/state

`Machines.Core` supplies state/step machines, separated-state composition and state-map simulation; `Machines.Trace.traceᴹ` is already generic on a **symmetric monoidal base V, monoidal distributivity, PureSub and Iteration.Elgot** (`Machines/Trace.agda:25–28,51–75`). It maps `Machine(A+X,B+X)` to `Machine(A,B)` by iterating the loop branch; yanking is proved at `:89–102`. This is a **coproduct-interface trace with internal state**, not a product-tensored synchronous stream trace or a bare UCSetup operation. SFun and relational machine carrier operators must be compared at their actual tensor and initialization boundaries rather than counted as new setup nodes.

## G11 — Already reusable game-level coupling/deferred sampling

`GamePlaying.Defer.AvgBisim` is parameterized by a rational seed distribution μ, two response kernels and a relation `(V→StE)→StL→Type`. Its averaged one-query law lifts through `runWith-avg` to equality of acceptance probabilities against every finite adaptive distinguisher (`GamePlaying/Defer.agda:43–78`). `GamePlaying.Defer.Run.DeferStep/runFrom-defer/run-defer` already supplies the distinct machine-Dₚ/runᴹ bridge, with explicit affine-seed equation `μ-const`, concluding `≈ₚ`, not `≈ᴹ` (`:50–108`). Actual consumer: `Examples/CoinToss/Ideal/Receiver/Machine.agda:229–245,347–362`.

`GamePlaying.Hop` already has `StepBisim/runWith-bisim`, `JoinStep/runWith-join`, `FLGP` and `hop-bound` (`:44–64,73–85,165–189`). `GamePlaying.Partial` has `SuperCert⊥`, `badProb⊥-bounded`, `Coupling⊥.FLGP⊥`, alphabet-relabelled and ordinary `StepBisim⊥/runWith⊥-bisim`, and partial `hop-bound` rules (`:94–107,147–172,238–275,323–342`). The exact partial bisimulation preserves every Bool→ℚ payoff. Hop bounds concern Pr1; bounding both SSP verdict masses needs a justified second-event adapter or a stronger exact all-payoff result. `SuperCert⊥` contains an invariant, nonnegative potential, bad-event lower bound, expected-step decrease and initial bound; error arithmetic alone does not manufacture it.

`SSP.Dist.Kernel.Samples/sample-⟦⟧` handles a normalized stochastic state map `ρ:S→Distℚ T` commuting with the **joint reply and successor state**, arbitrary dependent-channel `Calls`, and external-oracle state (`:239–245,285–295`). It is not the most general relational coupling. `SSP.Dist.Laws` supplies `coins-comm`, `coin-call`, `coin-comm`, `coin-drop`, `coin-flip`, `coin-xor`, `defer`, `stepwise` (`:32–76`); SSP OTP instantiates `defer draw` and proves actual source/quotient UC (`Examples/SSP/OneTimePad/Dist.agda:50–81,95–122`). SSP `Reduction` already supplies closed-game reduction and `≈ᵍ⇒≈ᵁ` (`:43–64,105–121`).

These are **existing A cores**, not missing coupling/lazy-sampling/fundamental-lemma frameworks. They do not themselves create a native source interpreter, a UC simulator, or target ancilla/test coverage. The useful remaining work is native typed-run/locality adapters preserving failure, joint state and each scheduler discipline; see package rows PK05–PK08 and candidate R2.

## G12 — Already parameterized choice structure

`ND.A.Relabelling` requires symmetric monoidal carrier, choice factors and functorial/monoidal site-relabeling realizations; NDA and relabel-security theorems follow (`ND/A.agda:59–205`). Operational PM lifetime selection and SSP queued-site picks are source-specific interpreters. This is not a distributive law of probability and powersets and not native task-PIOA scheduling.

## G13 — RS security plus independent availability

`UC.RandomSystems.Construction.Constructs ε f g=∃s.f∼ᴿ[ε]sub(s)g`, its zero/error/slack and composition theorems, and QRS `Witness` correspondence already exist. `Availability` has designated caps a:X→I,b:Y→I; `Available` compares the capped resources, `AvailableWithin` gives an error, `SecureConstruction`/`SecurelyConstructs εs εa` pair security with availability (`Availability.agda:38–76`). Transitivity requires compatibility of the middle designated modes and adds **security and availability errors independently** (`:107–132`). Security alone does not choose or preserve honest modes.

## G14 — Robust compilation characterisations

`UC.Robust.Compilation.Within` is generic: outcome-only preservation is characterised separately by `robust⇔outcomes` (`:44–59`); U-hyperproperty preservation by `≤UC⇔robustᵁ` (`:64–96`); environment-sensitive trace properties by `≈ᵁ⇔robustᵗ`, `≤UC⇔robustᵗ` (`:101–124`). `Along` uses existing setup-transfer hypotheses (`:130–165`). The trace-property reading retains the simulator before all properties/tests. The API characterises/derives preservation after a compiler and its conditions exist; it neither constructs backtranslation nor establishes native trace semantics.

## G15 — Existing event readers, rated audit transfer and capped saturation

`UC.Audit` takes a monoidal carrier, evaluation, Rates-graded subcategory and lower-real mass map. `AuditBound` uses explicitly permitted/certified contexts; `audit-carry` transports an ideal one-sided bound through a **rated fixed simulator** and `Absorbs`, giving `q↦ε(scale q r)+δ` for every positive numeric slack δ (`UC/Audit.agda:35–38,55–117,129–135`). This is not symmetric distance, automatic memory auditing, or a simulator chosen after δ.

`UC.Machine.EventBounds.Reader/BoundedAt/Saturated` already supplies PM event readers, original-context cap accounting and family saturation (`:64–69,90–134`). Precisely, `Saturated X ε = ∀polynomial p. ∃negligible ν. ∀n. X n (p n) (ε n (p n)+ν n)`. For `X=BoundedAt`, the selected ν bounds **all contexts under that cap**; it may depend on p. This is a fourth scope, distinct from local-context δ, within-test closure-uniform δ, and a single global δ.

Existing `UC.Quantitative.EventLift.hitsᵘ/agree/hits⇒upper` already lifts finite strategies to contextual completed-run event observations (`:49–101`). `Protocol.Safety` specializes `SuperCert⊥` at protocol kernels and proves completed-run hit≤prefix-hit (`:48–81`). `UC.Machine.StateEvent.Adequacy.stateRead-hit/stateBounded⇒hit/hit⇒stateBounded` and its family/saturated forms provide the actual **same-cap** protocol/PM bridge (`:185–236`). The reader's extra ask is explicitly accounted for; it is not free. `GamePlaying.Potential` already constructs rare-raise, uniform-guess, union and collision certificates (`:61–67,93–123,153–161`).

These are A coverage. Native adapters to other models must still supply lawful state/observable events, exact failure/stutter timing, initialization, readout and context/resource equations. Existing bad-then-dead and hidden-ghost controls (`StateEvent/HitTests.agda:97–106,154–172`) distinguish prefix stopping from completed-run flag reads and block arbitrary hidden-state annotation on a behavioral quotient. See PR-A5/PR-C2.

## G16 — Free syntax, occurrence decoration and coend actegories

`Categories.FreeMonoidal` already provides Mon/Symm variants, generator substitution and `freeStrongMonoidal` into a supplied target (`FreeMonoidal.agda:37–52,73–183,346–391,393–444,493–539`). Actual consumers are ITM, symbolic fine/certified and TaskPIOA wiring. Symbolic uses **Mon**. `FreeMonoidal.Decorated` supplies occurrence-dependent leaves, closure induction, and coherence via an occurrence bijection (`:34–100,156–187`), used by symbolic supplies/run-following. It does not create native binders, trace, copy or dynamic allocation.

`Categories.LocallyGraded.FreeActegory` constructs the actual coend category `∮`, its action, `η∮` and displayed flatten/unflatten maps (`:62–166,244–320,336–427`). The header explicitly calls the **universal free-actegory adjunction a Conjecture** (`:14`); do not promote it to an established universal property. Its graded-Kleisli specialization has an actual `bridge : StrongEquivalence K∮ K` and evaluation factorization (`FreeActegory/Kleisli.agda:60–147`), used by `Abstract2.Equivalence:191–199`.

## G17 — Generic monoidal carrier quotient and transport

`Categories.Category.Monoidal.Construction.Quotient` takes an equivalence containing ambient equality and closed under composition/tensor, changes hom equality while retaining representatives, and supplies `quotientF`; test descent needs a separate respect proof and has mono/epi comparison (`:33–105`). The actual contextual-kernel instances and security results are G7/PK09, not consequences of every quotient.

`...Construction.Transport` takes object indices, their unit/tensor, and chosen ambient isomorphisms; it conjugates structural maps and restricts homs via `Closed` identity/composition/tensor/structural-map laws, with strong monoidal `transportF/weaken` (`:1–20,51–52,185–247`). Actual ND-A/B and W carriers use it. This `Closed` record is not `Abstract2.WideSubcategory.SetupSubcategory`; their hypotheses must not be merged. Generic transport contains no tests/errors/security relation.

## G18 — G/Mealy versus SFun and exact support abstraction

The actual PM carrier is probabilistic Mealy/G `𝒢ₚᴹ`, sealed as `𝔾ᵒ` (`Machines/Base.agda:246–286`, `UC/Model/Seal.agda:43–44`). Generic G requires its traced structure and `TE.Laws Traced` (`Categories/GConstruction.agda:51–81`); operational `collapseᵀ` proves the paired-state/four-way dispatch reading (`Machines/Collapse.agda:113–151,198–234`). Machine state points here may be **effectful**. `Supp-traced` is an existing conditional depth-support theorem, not an error/security estimate (`Machines/Supp.agda:27–46`).

SFun is a **separate**, pure-initial-state, finite-word stateful-function category over `DiscreteMonad`, whose fields include commuting bind. Tensor uses disjoint input/output interfaces and paired states (`SFunM.agda:21–69,93–183`; `SFunM/Monoidal.agda:52–59,365–392`). A return/bind-respecting effect morphism gives exact word-run/evaluation preservation and a strong monoidal functor (`SFunM/Morphism.agda:22–147`). `SFunPossibility.agda:28–57` already instantiates it at `supp⊥ : Dist⊥→List`, exporting `suppᵉ`, `SFun-supp` and `SFun-supp-monoidal`. This is A infrastructure, not a missing support construction or an established SFun UCSetup.

`RationalDist/Support.agda:94–232` supplies positive support's return/bind/congruence laws. Existing `pad-supp-perfect/pad-not-perfect` and `close-supp-differs` (`:276–333`, with the preceding pad-support statement) show equal support can forget unequal probabilities and arbitrarily small positive statistical error can change support. Exact equality preservation is real; probability/error/security reflection is false. A full setup/test/grade bridge remains distinct. Detailed signatures and consumers are in [construction-generality-carrier-addendum.md](#appendix-c).

## G19 — Existing spans, worlds and relation-algebra operators

`Machines.Sim.Relational.RelSim` uses a common apex with pure projections, initialization and both step squares; composition/tensor/trace and `relsim⇒≈ᴹ` already exist (`:255–299`). Symbolic `Static.Relational` supplies its recipe/freshness/context instance. `Machines.Sim.RunFollowing` already has world-indexed `Stable/Following`, marginal and promotion laws, mixed Stable/Following closure, trace and `following-relsim` (`:92–112,301–347,408–456`); symbolic PairEnc/Role/Circuit/Supply are actual consumers. Arbitrary two-Following closure without synchronized worlds is not established, and no counterexample to that exact extension was supplied.

The installed category library's `RelsHasDagger/RelsDagger` and standard library's relation union/`Star`/`fold` are literal available carrier instances for one-shot Rel (S-R02/R03). RelSetup chooses **Cartesian-product tensor**, while Rel's categorical product/coproduct uses disjoint union. A compact product trace, sum-path feedback, initialized machine trace, and UC-security closure under any of them are different contracts. Existing carrier operators must be subtracted before proposing an abstraction; they do not manufacture one simulator for a union of separately secure branches.

## How to use this catalogue

A literature construction is class A only when its native inputs/laws actually fit one of these APIs and the relevant instance is present/proved. An existing generic carrier constructor may cover algebraic wiring while its native behavioral/full-abstraction theorem remains C/E. Conversely, a source-specific existing theorem is not 'missing' merely because it is not universal; classify its reuse as structured generalisation and record the source implementation.

No API above erases the need to prove native scheduling, fresh allocation, shared-state separation, dynamic session replication, operational hiding/linking, or probability-sensitive observation soundness. The negative controls are the PM bare/U separator, SSP illegal-return identity separator, correlated-versus-independent coin, local-versus-uniform negligible diagonal, and TaskPIOA scheduler/simulator quantifier boundary.

# Carrier/free/transport/support addendum

Normalized handoff from the completed `GenericConstructionCatalogue` scout, with Astra semantic scope corrections. Source paths are relative to the integration root's `src/`. The parent-supplied proof boundary is9f9be2a89; b24ae5f92 is the later docs-only head, **not a source discrepancy**. Declarations were inspected; no build/typecheck/test/lint/formatter was run. The owner additionally inspected `SFunPossibility.agda:19–57` and `RationalDist/Support.agda:276–333` directly.

These are **carrier/proof infrastructure**, not newly discovered UCSetup nodes. They must be subtracted before proposing a generic construction. Literature comparisons are the precise Mealy, Markov, Rel and trace contracts in the structural and symbolic/machine reports, not assertions that code automatically implements an entire native security model.

## CAPI4 — SFun, exact support abstraction and negative quantitative controls

1. **Construction/consumers/status.** Separate stateful finite-word SFun category, partial-rational and possibility instances, and a strong monoidal support abstraction are **already implemented**. They are not PM's G carrier and no SFun UCSetup was found in the bounded setup search.
2. **Primary contract/citation.** Actual source declarations below are primary evidence for this generic implementation. Literature structural comparisons: [Fritz2020](https://arxiv.org/abs/1908.07021), Definition2.1 and deterministic Definition10.1; [Abramsky–Coecke physical traces](https://arxiv.org/pdf/cs/0207057), §1, distinguishes relational sum/path and product/consistency feedback. These papers are not claimed to state the exact SFun Agda API.
3. **Native signature/laws.** `DiscreteMonad` includes return/bind/congruence and **commuting bind**, not any arbitrary noncommutative monad (`Categories/Monad/Discrete.agda:14–120`). `SFunᵉ` has pure initial state and `step:S×A→M(S×B)`; sequential composition pairs state. Equality is all finite-word evaluations. Tensor is disjoint interface sum with independent paired state; shared-state dispatch `[ _∣_ ]ᵏ` is not G/Elgot feedback (`CategoricalCrypto/SFunM.agda:21–69,93–183`, `SFunM/Monoidal.agda:52–59,365–392`).
4. **Actual API.** `SFunM/Morphism.agda:22–147` takes a return/bind-respecting monad morphism, postcomposes kernels, proves `θ-trace/θ-eval`, and exports `SFunᵉ-map` and strong monoidal `SFunᵉ-map-monoidal`. `SFunPartial.agda:25–49` uses `Dist⊥`. `SFunPossibility.agda:28–57` builds commutative possibility-list monad `Listᴰ`, instantiates `SFunMorphism Dist⊥ᴰ Listᴰ supp⊥ ... supp⊥-return supp⊥-bind`, and exports `suppᵉ`, `SFun-supp`, `SFun-supp-monoidal`.
5. **Classification.** **A** exact effect abstraction/finite-word functor. **D** reflection of probabilistic equality/security or continuity of support under positive statistical error. **E** a setup-level SFun/support security bridge unless separately constructed; no such bridge was established by the setup map.
6. **Exact effect instance.** `RationalDist/Support.agda:94–232` retains strictly positive rational entries; `supp-cong/return/bind` make the morphism. `supp⊥` removes `nothing` (`supp⊥-nothing=[]`), so loss of successful mass is intentional in the possibility readout. Exact distribution equality is preserved; this is not a metric/error-preserving claim.
7. **Positive/control examples.** Actual partial→possibility SFun functor is a literal consumer. Source `pad-supp-perfect` plus `pad-not-perfect` (`Support.agda:276–279`, preceding pad-support theorem) give equal supports but unequal biased-pad distributions. Symbolic report R04 gives the stronger explicit≥1/4 common-simulator obstruction for biased encryption.
8. **Concrete negative.** `close-supp-differs:298–333` constructs for every `0<ε≤1` distributions μ,ν which are ε-close against **all rewards in[0,1]** but have unequal supports. It proves a positive-error/continuity obstruction as well as support's inability to reflect weights. Source comments are backed by displayed theorem bodies, not a new verification run.
9. **Security/effects.** Carrier equality and finite-word evaluation commute with the exact effect morphism. Probability-sensitive tests and nonzero-error indistinguishability do not transport to exact possibility equality in general. A grade/test/simulator bridge is additional structure; this strong monoidal functor alone is not a UCSetupMorphism. Possibility support cannot measure quantitative availability.
10. **Obligations.** Reuse the existing morphism rather than reimplement support. A desired setup abstraction must specify its test map, ancillary coverage, admissible grades and one simulator. State its one-way possibilistic result and retain both source counterexamples; never infer cryptographic reflection from support. The unrelated `Protocol/Observe.agda:78–92` `evalC-support` lifts AllLeaves safety to OnSupport⊥, not termination or a probability bound.

## Remaining scope corrections

- The free-actegory **conjecture** is the identified open universal property; actual coend/action/bridge code must not be downgraded to planned work.
- SFun is auxiliary carrier evidence, not an omitted setup node or an alias for PM.
- Exact support abstraction is A, not merely a proposed finite-support construction. The missing item is its setup/test/security interpretation, and quantitative reflection is concretely false.
- All new semantic generalisations still need the native execution/test/ancilla and simulator obligations stated in the family reports. No checks were exercised by this addendum.
