<a id="appendix-d"></a>

# Appendix D — Astra structural primary-source synthesis

Original durable entry point: [construction-generality-structural-evidence.md](#appendix-d)

# Cross-model structural evidence (Astra-owned primary-source synthesis)

Read-only research. This artifact records independently inspected structural literature and deductions; it does not assert new repository theorems. Repository base contract was read at `src/CategoricalCrypto/UCSetup.agda:21–26` and `UC/Quantitative.agda:40–70` in `.worktrees/uc-setup-landscape`. Exact concrete API coverage belongs to the linked generic catalogue in the final report.

## Universal boundary

`UCSetup` contains a category C, a **monoidal grade category** I, a graded monad M:I→End(C), and a Setoid-valued presheaf E. It does not require a tensor on C. `QUCSetup` changes E to an Approx-valued presheaf over an ordered error algebra; it does not add distributions, convex combinations, couplings, clocks, state, fresh names, or a native transition system. `Standard2` is the special monoidal-carrier construction, not an axiom possessed by every setup.

[INFERENCE] A uniform countermodel schema is the unit-grade identity monad on any category with the constant terminal test presheaf. It is a UCSetup, and its observations can be indiscrete regardless of the carrier. Therefore an observational theorem cannot manufacture missing carrier operations. In the standard monoidal special case take the discrete category on natural numbers with tensor addition: there is no arrow 1→0 (discard) or 1→2 (copy). For total feedback, cartesian Set/FinSet cannot admit an unrestricted Conway fixed-point operator: Boolean negation has no fixed point. For a direct typed trace obstruction, the free symmetric monoidal category on one generator f:A⊗U→B⊗U has no A→B arrow (no U is available), so trace cannot be derived just from its monoidal laws. These are mathematical countermodels, not repository-run tests.

## X1 — Guarded instantaneous feedback / iteration (C; unrestricted version D)

**Primary contract.** Goncharov–Schröder, *Guarded traced categories*, FoSSaCS 2018, [arXiv:1802.08756](https://arxiv.org/abs/1802.08756), inspected v1. Definition 1 (`goncharov2018.txt:180–193`) supplies a symmetric monoidal carrier plus partially guarded hom subsets Hom•(A⊗B,C⊗D), with unit, vacuous tensor, composition, parallel and derived weakening laws. Definition 25 (`440–455`) supplies a guarded trace

`tr_U : Hom•((A⊗U)⊗B, C⊗(D⊗U)) → Hom•(A⊗B,C⊗D)`

satisfying typed versions of vanishing, superposing, sliding and yanking. Definition 30 and Proposition 32 (`501–546`) give the equivalent guarded Conway iteration in the cocartesian case, including fixpoint/naturality/dinaturality/codiagonal; uniformity is a separately stated principle. Metric examples require completeness and contraction, not a bare error-valued observation.

**Generality/status.** Not supplied by UCSetup or QUCSetup axioms. Existing reactive execution/GoI feedback can be a concrete consumer, but one must identify *its* guardedness and law set before calling it an instantiation. Candidate further consumers are native IPDL cyclic linking and CSP network wiring; neither their missing linker nor native equivalence correspondence is obtained from Standard2 composition.

**Minimal additional data.** A specified symmetric monoidal carrier or graded Kleisli carrier; decidable or proof-relevant guardedness invariant closed by the listed operations; a trace satisfying the defined laws; compatibility of the grade action with that trace. To lift observation/security results, supply a **test-closing operation** that reifies an admissible feedback context as a tester, with an execution/pullback equation and ancilla compatibility. This is concrete context data to prove, not the bare hypothesis 'trace preserves UC'.

**Controls.** Boolean negation refutes total fixed-point/trace structure on all cartesian functions. A local positive example is a one-step delayed register; zero-delay identity and one-step delay cannot be identified by time-observing tests. Guardedness does not justify changing one native scheduler into another.

**Error/security.** Exact contextual replacement may extend when the closing-context equation is proved. Arbitrary ε-nonexpansiveness of unbounded iteration is false: [INFERENCE] repeat Bernoulli(ε) trials until success versus a never-successful trial. Their one-call difference is ε, but eventual-success observations separate by 1; after n trials the gap is 1−(1−ε)^n≤nε. Hence bounded-use/query accounting or contractive summability is required; the existing quantitative composition API supplies only the later error arithmetic. Reflection also needs representative simulator/grade closure, not trace laws alone.

**Proof obligations.** Identify the exact tensor and loop discipline per consumer; prove operational trace laws including identity; implement context closing; prove finite-use or contraction bound; prove the compiler/linker square independently of observation coarsening.

## X2 — Initialized state / uniform stored-state feedback (C; do not conflate with X1)

**Primary contract.** Bonchi–Di Lavore–Román, *Effectful Mealy machines*, [arXiv:2410.10627v4](https://arxiv.org/abs/2410.10627v4), 25 June 2026 version. Definitions 2.2/2.4 (`bonchidilavore2025.pdf:155–186`) give a copy-discard inclusion V→P and an effectful triple V→P→C: cartesian values, monoidal local effects, premonoidal global effects with central image. Definition 3.2 and Proposition 3.4 (`201–225`) define machine `(S,i:I→S,step:S⊗A→S⊗B)` and cascade by product/tensor of **separate** states. Definitions 3.5/3.6 (`227–245`) define value-map state homomorphisms preserving initialization/step and zig-zag bisimulation; Proposition 3.10 makes that relation a congruence. Theorem 3.18 additionally uses weak-pullback conditions for coincidence with coalgebraic bisimulation.

Definition 4.1 and Theorem 4.4 (`395–455`) provide uniform feedback

`fbk : P(I,S) × F(S⊗A,S⊗B) → F(A,B)`.

Its laws are tightening, strength, joining, and uniformity under value-state homomorphisms. Remark 4.2 explicitly distinguishes feedback from trace: the latter additionally has **yanking**, which makes the loop instantaneous. Stored state is not an instantaneous traced feedback operator.

**Generality/status.** A carrier-level construction on structured monoidal/effectful data, not on arbitrary setups. The repository already has generic Mealy/G and separate SFun/state-machine infrastructure, plus stochastic `Samples/sample-⟦⟧`, machine-run `Defer.Run`, partial bisimulation and PM/protocol event adequacy. These are A cores, not missing frameworks. The useful remaining target is **native state/run/locality adapters** for SSP, TaskPIOA and IPDL, preserving their actual initializers, failure/scheduler semantics and chosen tensors. Internal product states do not make the external interface tensor cartesian.

**Minimal extra assumptions.** Explicit initializer, state product/coherence, step semantics, chosen class of state maps; if global state is hidden, a centrality/separation discipline or explicitly premonoidal semantics. Value-state homomorphisms preserve initial and transition maps. Relational/coupling simulation needs a separately lawful relation lifting and observation factorization. Those are step-level data, not a UC-soundness conclusion supplied as an axiom.

**Controls.** [INFERENCE] Two commands writing different bits into the *same* hidden cell fail interchange: write0;write1 ends in 1, the reverse in 0. Separate state cells do commute when neither command reads/writes the other. Mere existence of a product type does not justify a tensor of shared-state computations. An isomorphism of uninitialized state carriers says nothing about which state is initially selected; initialization has to be transported.

**Security/error.** State homomorphism/bisimulation is a proof witness, not an adversarial simulator. Once it implies equality of every admissible run, it enters hom/test/kernel soundness and the existing transfer API; reflection is not automatic. Approximate coupling with n relevant calls accumulates the stated error, not automatically one ε for the lifetime.

**Proof obligations.** Align initializers, deadlock/failure and verdict semantics; prove state renaming/frame lemmas; prove trace/run semantics functorial; establish test/ancilla coverage; preserve the distinction between SFun's interleaving tensor and product-tensored synchronous histories.

## X3 — Copy/discard versus copying a computation (C; unrestricted natural copying D)

**Primary contract.** Fritz, *A synthetic approach to Markov kernels, conditional independence and theorems on sufficient statistics*, Adv. Math. 2020, [arXiv:1908.07021](https://arxiv.org/abs/1908.07021). Definition 2.1 (`fritz2020.txt:404–463`) requires symmetric monoidal structure with compatible commutative comonoids `copy_X:X→X⊗X`, `del_X:X→I`, and natural discard. Definition 10.1 (`1613–1634`) defines **deterministic** maps by copy preservation. Copy is deliberately **not natural for arbitrary stochastic morphisms**. Bonchi et al. Definition 2.2 weakens natural discard too, accommodating partial computations.

**Generality/status.** No copy/discard structure follows from a setup. Positive consumers: one-shot Rel has diagonal relations and deletion; deterministic value/state components inside SSP/TaskPIOA/IPDL have ordinary copying. Whole PM/SSP converter interfaces are not thereby cartesian. Native probabilistic transitions can support a copy-discard category but partial failure prevents assuming Markov natural discard.

**Minimal data/laws.** Distinguished deterministic-value subcategory and identity-on-objects strong symmetric monoidal inclusion into computation category; specified copy/discard comonoids. Only deterministic maps commute with copying. If arbitrary computation discard is required, impose genuine totality/normalization, not merely a terminal-looking interface.

**Counterexample.** A fair coin copied after sampling yields `(0,0)` and `(1,1)` only; two independent executions also yield `(0,1)` and `(1,0)` with total probability 1/2. A diverging/aborting computation followed by discard is not the no-op if tests distinguish absence of a verdict. This directly controls deferred-sampling and state/session-splitting proposals.

**Security/error.** Copying sampled classical values may be exact; duplicating a cryptographic resource can expose correlations and is not an automatic UC-preserving operation. Independent tensor replacement uses the established laws of the relevant structured model and its admitted contexts; shared-state reuse needs a separate construction theorem. No probability-sensitive reflection follows from relational copying.

## X4 — Conditioning/disintegration and randomness pushback (C; universal versions D/E)

**Primary contract.** Fritz Definition 11.5 (`2413–2447`): for each `f:A→X⊗Y`, a conditional `f|X:X⊗A→Y` with factorization `f(x,y|a)=f|X(y|x,a) f(x|a)`. Existence is extra: finite stochastic kernels and standard-Borel stochastic kernels satisfy it, arbitrary measurable Stoch need not (Examples 11.3/11.18). Proposition 11.15 (`2702–2705`) says unconditional uniqueness of conditionals forces every pair of parallel arrows equal. Definition 11.19 and Example 11.20 (`2822–2855`) give randomness pushback `f=g∘(ψ⊗id)` with a deterministic `g` and initial random seed `ψ`; for FinStoch, sample a random function using the product of per-input distributions.

**Generality/status.** Coupling/deferred-sampling candidates in SSPᵈ, finite TaskPIOA, finite IPDL and RS can consume finite rational stochastic structure. This is not supplied by QUC's Approx tests. Native adaptive interactive behavior needs a persistent joint seed/state, not just one-call marginal factorization. No assertion here that a setup-level conditioning API exists.

**Minimal data/laws.** A normalized stochastic/copy-discard semantics; finite rational distributions or another explicitly closed measurable class; existence of conditional kernels with correct marginals, coherent execution only up to the appropriate almost-sure relation; seed factorization whose evaluation preserves the joint protocol trace and allocation lifetime. Conditioning on an impossible event does not determine a unique kernel.

**Controls.** The copied-coin versus independent-coins example refutes replacing shared randomness by independent marginal samplers. Off-support conditional branches can differ arbitrarily while inducing the same joint distribution, so quotienting all kernels by an informal 'the conditional' equality is unsound. If abort mass is normalized away, conditional distributions can conceal availability loss; the full subdistribution/cemetery outcome must be tracked.

**Security/error.** Exact joint-distribution equations can be sound under all finite adaptive tests; one-call equality is insufficient. Approximate conditioning can amplify errors by an inverse event probability and needs a positive lower bound; no general nonexpansiveness theorem follows from QUC. Probability-sensitive security reflection through support fails; the existing exact SFun support abstraction and source lossiness theorems are separately catalogued in G18/CAPI4.

## X5 — Port connection / composition-order invariance (C; fixed-point selection alone D)

**Primary contract.** Matt–Maurer–Portmann–Renner–Tackmann, *Toward an algebraic theory of systems*, TCS 2018, [arXiv:1609.04293](https://arxiv.org/abs/1609.04293), inspected v2. Definition 3.1 (`matt2018.txt:702–738`) gives finite boundary labels λ(s), disjoint parallel composition, allowed connections Γ(s), and connection γ deleting its two boundary ports. Definition 3.2 (`758–766`) separates admissibility reordering, connection commutation, and compatibility with parallel composition. Theorem 4.6 derives composition-order invariance from unique fixed points plus allowed reordering; Theorem 5.8 instead uses suitable ordered domains/least fixed points; Theorem 6.6 supplies the causal-system instance.

**Generality/status.** Useful shared target for native IPDL private linking and CSP confined networks (and planned RSIM/causal systems as controls, not constructed consumers). Repository categorical composition/tensor already gives algebraic substitution after a lawful carrier exists, but does not prove that an operational gluing evaluator is that composition. IPDL already has concrete hidden-bit, identity, flattening and seeded-feedback linker squares; the unproved extension is a general typed/hygienic linker and source setup/security bridge. CSP is explicitly in progress.

**Minimal structural assumptions.** Finite typed boundaries; disjoint renaming; private-wire equivalence classes; admissibility invariant closed by gluing; a semantics with either unique guarded solutions or a least-solution construction satisfying the actual domain hypotheses. Prove flattened wiring semantics invariant under associativity, aliases, and permutation; then reuse Standard2 and contextual quotient APIs. This is operational content, not the circular hypothesis 'composition is sound'.

**Concrete primary counterexample.** The paper's §4.2 example (`958–972`) uses Boolean system outputs `(¬x1,¬x2,x1)` and consistently chooses 0 when a feedback equation has two solutions. Connecting i2→o1 then i1→o2 leaves visible o3=0; reversing connection order leaves o3=1. Thus **consistent fixed-point choice alone is insufficient**, even when every individual step looks deterministic.

**Security/error.** Once an exact operational linker square and test-closing equation hold, ordinary generic substitution/security transfer is reusable; port hiding itself does not produce a simulator or ensure its uniformity. Timing, scheduling and divergence observations must be chosen before a quotient. Quantitative loop bounds are subject to X1's amplification control.

## X6 — Convex/Wasserstein lifting, not just an error presheaf (C; arbitrary-QUC version D)

**Primary contract.** Mardare–Panangaden–Plotkin, *Free complete Wasserstein algebras*, [arXiv:1802.07366](https://arxiv.org/abs/1802.07366), archived companion text. Section 2 (`mardare2018-companion.txt:202–250`) defines `W_p(μ,ν)=inf_γ(∫d(x,y)^p dγ)^(1/p)` over couplings with given marginals, for Radon measures with finite p-moment, p≥1. Section 3 (`506–527,610–614`) requires barycentric operations `+_r` (projection, idempotence, skew commutativity/associativity) and the quantitative convexity law `d(x+_r y,x′+_r y′)^p≤r d(x,x′)^p+(1−r)d(y,y′)^p`. Theorem 3.3 gives the free finite-support Wasserstein algebra; Theorem 3.6 gives the free complete one over a complete metric space. Completeness/Radon/moment conditions are genuine extra assumptions, not universal QUC facts.

**Repository/generality.** QUC supplies an Approx-valued **test presheaf**, not a metric, convex operations, distribution monad, or couplings on all native states. Finite rational probability layers in SSP and TaskPIOA/IPDL are positive consumers for a restricted p=1 finite-distribution instance; current setups do not thereby become full real-valued Wasserstein-algebra models. Existing rational error/hybrid APIs remain A; an actual convex-metric lifting and its native readout interpretation are C, not inferred from those APIs.

**Minimal structure and laws.** Specify the state/output metric, convex kernel semantics, coupling/marginal operations and tensor/bind compatibility; choose finite rational support or prove the stated measure/completeness conditions. Supply nonexpansive **admitted test evaluations**. A coupling cost bounds a test advantage only when the test's sensitivity is controlled; this is data to verify for the chosen observation presheaf.

**Controls [INFERENCE].** Discrete two-point metric space has no midpoint operation satisfying the Wasserstein convexity inequality: its midpoint must be one endpoint, and comparison with the other idempotent endpoint forces `1≤1/2`. Hence a metric alone does not supply convex choice. On real-valued outputs, δ₀ and δ_ε have W₁-distance ε, while the predicate “output equals 0” separates them with advantage1; therefore arbitrary distinguishers cannot be declared 1-Lipschitz. Rational finite support is also not closed under arbitrary real convex coefficients or metric completion.

**Security/proof obligations.** Identify exactly which outcome metric controls which native tests, then instantiate generic QUC and test restriction/refinement. Bounded-Lipschitz security may be weaker than full statistical UC, with reflection requiring a test-separation theorem. Preserve normalization/abort mass and distinguish convex mixing from nondeterministic joins (the latter has separate distributivity obstructions). Prioritise the finite rational instance only if two consumers need this metric rather than their already available total-variation/expectation bounds.

## Research priority consequence

The best common layer is a small collection of **distinct** structural interfaces with concrete run/test theorems, not one universal 'trace/choice/state' superclass. Highest-value two-consumer work is finite state/coupling reasoning for SSPᵈ+TaskPIOA/IPDL; a common disciplined linker contract for IPDL+CSP; and test/ancilla-density transfer for compiler images. Stored-state feedback, guarded instantaneous feedback, private-name hiding, internal nondeterminism and grade choice must remain separate until their semantic laws are proved.

<a id="appendix-e"></a>

# Appendix E — Exact reusable-candidate signatures

Original durable entry point: [construction-generality-reusable-candidates.md](#appendix-e)

# Reusable construction candidates and control obligations

Astra-owned mathematical synthesis, read-only. All newly proposed statements below are **[INFERENCE]**, not existing repository theorems. Exact APIs already present are in [construction-generality-generic-apis.md](#appendix-b); primary structural contracts and controls are in [construction-generality-structural-evidence.md](#appendix-d). These candidates have been consolidated against all six family inventories; their existing A cores are explicitly subtracted.

## R1. Two-budget designated-mode construction on arbitrary QUCSetup — proposed B

Given ordinary grade arrows a:X→I and b:Y→I, protocols f:A→T_X B and g:A→T_Y B, define

`Avail[εa](a,b;f,g) := sub(a)∘f ≈ᵁ[εa] sub(b)∘g`

and pair it with `Witness εs f g`. This is well-typed on **any QUCSetup**, because both capped terms have grade I; it does not require T_I B≅B or a tensor on the computation category. Reflexivity/transitivity use the existing `ctx-*` and `QWitness.at-trans`; transitivity with the same middle cap adds the security budgets and availability budgets independently. If middle caps are hom-equal, `sub-resp-≈` gives the zero-error compatibility step. A setup with no cap X→I simply has no such designated mode; the theorem does not create one.

- **Consumers:** QRS availability, QPM (and quantitative family lifts once their caps are supplied).
- **What is new:** a small generic packaging/lemma around existing QUC arithmetic; not a new operational availability semantics. Current RS `no-adversary` additionally removes the standard unitor and compares behavioral observations. Its identification with this generic definition needs the actual QRS full-abstraction/zero/slack theorem.
- **Control:** a simulator can answer an adversarial control request in an ideal-resource attack mode while the designated honest mode would not. Security-only simulation therefore cannot supply cap compatibility. Likewise, availability edges through two differently behaving caps on the middle resource do not compose without relating those modes.
- **Obligation:** package the generic theorem, identify the native RS instance, and retain the fixed simulator and **two** errors. PR-B1 already supplies a concrete incompatible-cap counterexample from the actual false/true-mode resources and the existing security-without-availability example; these are controls, not missing research. Do not label arbitrary liveness/fairness as availability unless the readout actually observes it.

## R2. Native-run adapters to existing sampling simulations — C, with substantial A core

The useful common interface exposes a constructor-faithful native experiment encoding, state/init, dependent reply types, and an exact run square into existing `SSP.Dist.Kernel.Samples`/`sample-⟦⟧`. `Samples` already handles a normalized stochastic state map ρ:S→Distℚ T commuting with the **joint reply and next state**, while retaining arbitrary external-oracle state. Do not add another finite-run induction. The adapter transfers the existing result to equality of all native outcome payoffs, preserving the cemetery/no-verdict outcome.

- **Consumers:** SSPᵈ and TaskPIOA; IPDL residual interpreter is a third concrete consumer. PM machine simulation provides another related but not identical carrier.
- **Already present:** `SSP.Dist.Kernel.Samples`, `sample-⟦⟧`; `GamePlaying.Defer.AvgBisim/runWith-avg`; `GamePlaying.Defer.Run.DeferStep/runFrom-defer/run-defer` at Dₚ machine-run semantics with affine seed; partial-game `StepBisim⊥/runWith⊥-bisim`; TaskPIOA deterministic state simulation/product trace-match; generic machine relational simulations. These are genuine A cores, with different input signatures, not a missing universal probability abstraction.
- **Proposed common improvement:** TaskPIOA `tick` can be adapted using query=task and reply=Maybe output, swapping reply/state order and embedding its total distribution into Dist⊥. Disabled-task stutter is Dirac(nothing,state), not failure; existing `Simulation.tick-match` covers Dirac state maps. Add native fixed-word trace/payoff adequacy plus input/product locality for stochastic state maps. IPDL already has typed turn/resource and `compileTree/execution/agreement`; do not re-propose its existing finite adaptive adequacy theorem. Its general stochastic linker/ancilla bridge remains separate.
- **Controls:** copied coin vs two independent coins (joint distribution); same hidden cell writes0/1 (semantic independent tensor fails), disabled task must stutter rather than abort, and initialization must match. SSProve's syntactic linking/interchange laws can hold even with shared global state; the shared-cell control targets semantic command interchange/independent-state separation, not those syntactic identities.
- **Obligations:** prove the two native interface/run adapters and preserve all outcome payoffs; compare homogeneous Q,R/Bool finite strategies, dependent SSP Calls, and Dₚ machine runs rather than identifying them. Upgrade to U only with product/wiring closure and admitted-test coverage, while TaskPIOA's residual scheduler stays precommitted. Approximate versions need per-step potential/query accounting before generic hybrid addition. A stochastic map simulation is not the most general relational coupling; record that exact scope.

## R3. Test-density/ancilla-basis transfer — C, extending existing A transfer alternatives

`Transfer.transfer⁺` already reduces UC preservation to U-kernel preservation. Existing model proofs discharge it differently: W inclusion uses all-object image coverage; ITM uses enough image duals; SSP uses finite test density. A reusable contextual density interface can expose **actual translated test data**, not merely assume `Preserves-≈ᵁ` again.

One quantitative shape: for each target ancilla/test and ε>0, construct a source ancillary test (or finite tester), common to the compared protocols, together with a run-evaluation comparison whose total slack is ≤2ε. Exact source equality plus arbitrarily small target slack gives target equality under the target's specified closed/all-positive observation relation. If the target is defined by an error relation, explicitly state which zero/closure principle permits the limit step; metric completeness is not automatically needed, and open-zero is not all-positive.

- **Consumers:** SSP→PM[wb], ITM→PM; later symbolic feasible backtranslation and IPDL legal testers.
- **Controls:** PM `reveal-≈ℰ` plus `¬gradeStableᵒ` refutes replacing all this with bare-kernel preservation. Target simulator density is not implied by tester density. The old SSP illegal-return wire separator prevents silently changing target from PM[wb] to PM.
- **Obligations:** expose image-ancilla conjugation (`Standard2.Morphism.at-image` already exists), identify a smaller testing/ancilla basis; preserve the one simulator chosen before target context and ε. Reflection needs actual simulator lifting/fullness as separate data. Do not add a new generic theorem if the same statement is already `surjective⇒preserves-≈ᵁ`.

## R4. Disciplined typed open-network linking — C/E; negative unrestricted proposals D

Give a native typed-boundary gluing interface with explicit private names/wire aliases, operational transition/readout semantics and a closure invariant. Prove network flattening/renaming/gluing equations **operationally**; only then instantiate free/quotient/Standard2 machinery.

- **Consumers:** IPDL and CSP source theories; neither is claimed to be an implemented setup-level common consumer today. IPDL has actual concrete hidden-bit, identity and seeded-feedback squares; CSP remains implementation-in-progress. Planned causal/RSIM are controls, not part of the constructed census.
- **Common data:** boundaries, admissible connections, fresh disjoint namespace embeddings, private-wire equivalence classes, closedness under plugging, semantic invariance under transparent aliases and associative flattening. The dynamics differ: IPDL persistent single-assignment quiescence versus CSP rendezvous/τ/nondeterminism. Do not force them through one evaluator.
- **Controls:** Matt et al.'s Boolean two-feedback counterexample refutes arbitrary consistent fixed-point selection; IPDL demand-order-exposing compilation refutes a different interface than push/poll; CSP cross-alphabet synchronization refutes unrestricted regrouping; hiding may create divergence. Scope extrusion needs freshness.
- **Obligations:** prove each native linker square; only hide genuinely private classes; distinguish termination, deadlock and observation absence; prove native context correspondence and test-closing. Source-carrier equality must be justified before setup-morphism security transfer.

## R5. Scheduler information and schedule-map interface — C; quantifier exchange D/E

Expose an allowed scheduler as concrete data with its observation filtration/information access, task alphabet and map of schedule prefixes. Composition retains interleaving/shuffle information. Disabled tasks have a specified no-action semantics. A schedule transformer must preserve the information restriction and synchronize component actions lawfully.

- **Consumers:** actual TaskPIOA and source-specific PM/SSP choice interpreters, though their current schedules/grade effects differ. Native CSP scheduler resolution can be a later consumer, not an automatic probability assignment.
- **Controls:** a hidden fair bit and an oblivious 0/1 guess task give success1/2; a state-peeking scheduler gives1. A first-message-wins receiver separates two task interleavings. Native `∀E ρ.∃ρ′` cannot be swapped to one UC simulator without a uniform constructive transformer; a logical payoff matrix is a quantifier nonimplication, not by itself a formal native-model counterexample.
- **Obligations:** define scheduler information, product/shuffle, stuttering, and uniformity separately. A/B grade relabeling is already generic; prove the actual native scheduler interpretation before naming it demonic/angelic/task semantics. Probability/powerset distributivity requires its own convex-semialgebra laws and respects published no-go conditions.

## R6. Nominal allocation and session/state separation — C/E, not arbitrary grade rename

Use typed namespaces with finite support/permutation action (or an explicit equivalent binder representation), freshness and alpha-equivalence, substitution avoiding capture, allocation semantics, and scope extrusion with freshness side conditions. For session splitting, add state ownership/locality and an operational factorization into independent session state; dynamic replication additionally needs a finite-prefix/resource model and runtime closure.

- **Consumers:** symbolic roles/applied-pi correspondence and IPDL private channels; SSP locations and ITM session routers are related consumer domains but not identical binders.
- **Controls:** capturing a free channel changes communication; reusing a pad/state cell across sessions exposes correlations; a finite fresh namespace can be exhausted; arbitrarily many sessions do not follow from binary tensor. Constructor tags solve static disjointness, not arbitrary runtime allocation.
- **Obligations:** identify actual local naming APIs and distinguish finite occurrence naming from unbounded nominal semantics. Prove renaming equivariance, scope/extrusion, allocation persistence and simulator namespace transport. Joint/global state wrappers need their native ideal-resource construction theorem, not a slogan that UC composition handles shared state.

## R7. Guarded feedback / bounded iteration, kept separate from stored-state feedback — C

Reuse actual `Machines.Trace` when its coproduct/PureSub/Elgot inputs fit. Generalizations to other native theories require explicit guardedness/trace or stored-state feedback laws as in the structural appendix. Exact source equality and finite-context observation closure are separate from quantitative iteration bounds.

- **Consumers:** machine/PM feedback and IPDL linked cycles; CSP guarded recursion is a candidate only after the precise coalgebra/equality is chosen.
- **Controls:** Boolean negation prevents unrestricted cartesian fixed points; delayed register does not satisfy instantaneous yanking; repeated ε-probability trials amplify to almost1; unseeded IPDL cycle remains blocked rather than being assigned an arbitrary fixed point.
- **Obligations:** name the tensor and equality at every point, distinguish new internal-state allocation from an instantaneous loop, prove guarded closure and context reification, state finite-use/contractive error bounds. No claim that all seven candidates are new: their A cores have been explicitly subtracted.

# Appendix F — Probabilistic/reactive source inventory

Original durable entry point: [construction-generality-probreactive.md](#appendix-f)

# Probabilistic/reactive construction generality: PM, RS/QRS, and quantitative families

## Scope, evidence boundary, and reading convention

This is read-only primary-literature and inspected-source research for the PM/RS slice, not an implementation or verification report. No source/docs changes, builds, typechecks, tests, linters, formatters, or git validation were performed. The parent reports docs-only current commit `b24ae5f92` and unchanged source proof boundary `9f9be2a89`; those identifiers are not independently checked here. Exact code references below resolve in `/Users/andre/IOHK/categorical-crypto/.worktrees/uc-setup-landscape`, abbreviated **R**. Code paths beginning `CC/` abbreviate `R/src/CategoricalCrypto/`; **P/** abbreviates `R/docs/kb/sources/papers/`. PDF line numbers are the `read` tool's extracted-text anchors, not printed-page numbers. Formulas below restore ordinary mathematical notation where extraction loses glyphs; theorem/definition numbering is that of the inspected archived edition.

The common brief, source census, completed implementation/API scouts, owner-corrected [construction-generality-generic-apis.md](#appendix-b), and parent structural evidence [construction-generality-structural-evidence.md](#appendix-d) govern the classifications. In particular: arbitrary `UCSetup` has a category C, a monoidal grade category, a graded monad and a test presheaf; C need not be monoidal. `QUCSetup` enriches **tests** in an error-indexed Approx structure. It does not add probabilities, convex mixtures, coupling, conditioning, copy/discard, stored state, or feedback to C. Native PM/RS operations need their own operational/observational bridge.

**Bounded standardness.** “Standard” here means an actual definition or theorem in the seven named primary works below, with the relevant repository contracts also inspected. This is a census of their foundational operations/proof principles and their applicability to the mapped nodes, not a claim to exhaust every random-system paper or every cryptographic protocol. Arbitrary cipher/MAC/hash/protocol recipes are deliberately excluded. A rows provide coverage but are **not new construction candidates**. New deductions and counterexamples assembled from source facts are labelled **[INFERENCE]**. Source-present is not a new typechecking claim.

## 1. Primary corpus and exact coverage

| Key and public primary citation | Inspected edition and precise contracts used | Meaningful exclusions / limits |
|---|---|---|
| **M02** — Ueli Maurer, *Indistinguishability of Random Systems*, EUROCRYPT 2002, LNCS 2332, 110–132. [Author publication](https://crypto.ethz.ch/publications/Maurer02.html), [PDF](https://crypto.ethz.ch/publications/files/Maurer02.pdf). | `P/maurer2002.pdf`: §3, Defs. 1–4 sources/automata/conditional-kernel random systems/beacons, URFs, URPs and ROs (`77–115`); Defs. 5–7 event-tagged equality, conditioning and adjoining events (`117–140`); Def. 8 cascade (`151–153`); invocation and Lemma 4 (`173–179`); Defs. 9–10 advantage and Lemma 5 wrapper/hybrid bounds (`198–211`); Def. 11, Lemmas 6–7 and Thm. 1 bad-event bounds (`219–246`); Thm. 2 adaptivity removal (`248–264`); Thm. 3 independent-event product (`265–281`); Def. 12 query-bounded quasirandomness (`295ff`). | Not a computational/PPT definition merely because a query cap is present. Applications in later sections are not enumerated. Native systems are normalized, responding conditional kernels; they are not automatically the whole possibly nonterminating PM carrier. |
| **MPR07** — Ueli Maurer, Krzysztof Pietrzak, Renato Renner, *Indistinguishability Amplification*, CRYPTO 2007, LNCS 4622, 130–149. [Author publication](https://crypto.ethz.ch/publications/MaPiRe07.html). | `P/maurer2007.pdf`: Defs. 2–3 random systems/equality and independence convention (`94–118`); Defs. 5–7 advantage/lifetime mixture, Lemmas 2–3 (`138–196`); Defs. 9–12 monotone binary outputs, masking, restricted equivalence, hitting probability and transcript distance, Lemmas 4–5 (`198–255`); Def. 13 neutralizing construction, Lemma 6 independent games, Thm. 1 product bound (`340–416`); feed-forward Cor. 1, quasigroup Def. 14/Cor. 2, permutation cascade Def. 15/Cor. 3 (`418–453`); Thm. 2 adaptive-to-nonadaptive transcript/hybrid bound (`461–494`). | Information-theoretic amplification is not an unrestricted computational theorem; the paper explicitly warns about this. No general efficient maximal-coupling algorithm is claimed. |
| **MR11** — Ueli Maurer, Renato Renner, *Abstract Cryptography*, ICS 2011, author edition 1–21. The paper does not define the repository's exact local-negligible or small-image family test equalities. |

Def. 3 uses a common displayed ε for both clauses; separate budgets are a faithful refinement, not the paper's displayed definition. |
